ARCSPLIT.PCFShow detailsARCSPLIT.PCF
  • 4 processing steps

Processing steps

Step 1

ATTXTR. Report the attitude in ORBEX format Programs: ATTXTR. The Bernese program(s) called are ATTXTR.ATTXTR: Report the attitude from GNSSATTI, $YD+0

Step 2

ARCSPLIT. Arc splitting for reprocessing solution Programs: ORBGEN, DEFXTR. The Bernese program(s) called are DEFXTR, ORBGEN, POLUPD, STDELE. Before it can start, it waits for PID 001 (ATTXTR) to finish.DEFXTR: IGSFIN1D_$YSS+0: ASPLIT FOR $K ORBIT SOLUTIONORBGEN: IGSFIN1D_$YD+0: ORBIT CHECKPOLUPD: Extract current day $YD+0 from a priori pole fileSTDELE: ORBIT OVERLAP

Step 3

SPLT_SAV. Save result files of ARCSPLIT.PCF to savedisk Programs:. No Bernese program is called in this step. Before it can start, it waits for PID 010 (ARCSPLIT) to finish.

Orbit overlaps

Step 4

ORBPM. Execute ORBPM. The Bernese program(s) called are ORBCMP. Before it can start, it waits for PID 020 (SPLT_SAV) to finish.ORBCMP: ORBCMP Orbit overlaps

BASTST.PCFShow detailsBASTST.PCF

Does GPSEST runs baseline by baseline for trouble shooting. BASTST BPE processing summary file name: BASyyssss.PRC

  • 5 processing steps

Delete specific files

Step 1

BAS_DEL. Delete old files concerning BASTST.PCF. No Bernese program is called in this step.

Compute baseline solutions and extract relevant information

Step 2

BASESTAP. Prepare parallel GPSEST run for baseline-wise analysis. No Bernese program is called in this step. Before it can start, it waits for PID 001 (BAS_DEL) to finish.

Step 3

BASEST_X. Execute GPSEST for baseline-wise analysis. The Bernese program(s) called are GPSEST. Before it can start, it waits for PID 101 (BASESTAP) to finish.GPSEST: $(CLUSTER) BASTST_$YSS+0_$(CLUSTER)

Step 4

RESRMS. check the residuals for outliers and remove them Programs: RESRMS. The Bernese program(s) called are RESRMS. Before it can start, it waits for PID 102 (BASEST_X) to finish.RESRMS: BASTST_$YSS+0

Create processing summary file

Step 5

BAS_SUM. Create BASTST BPE processing summary file. The Bernese program(s) called are GPSXTR. Before it can start, it waits for PID 111 (RESRMS) to finish.GPSXTR: BASTST_$YSS+0

CLKVER.PCFShow detailsCLKVER.PCF
  • 2 processing steps

Processing steps

Step 1

CLKVERAP. Station-wise zero-difference data screening Programs:. No Bernese program is called in this step.

Step 2

CLKVER_P. Station-wise zero-difference data screening: 1. GPSEST to create a residual files 2. RESRMS to screen residual files 3. RESCHK To detect bad stations 4. SATMRK to mark outliers. The Bernese program(s) called are GPSEST. Before it can start, it waits for PID 101 (CLKVERAP) to finish.GPSEST: $YD+0, CLOCK ESTIMATION, SOLUTION CODE+PHASE, GRE

FCLKGNSS.PCFShow detailsFCLKGNSS.PCF

Final GNSS clock estimation based on ADDNEQ2

  • 156 processing steps

Processing steps

Step 1

BPE_INI. The script performs this processing step. No Bernese program is called in this step.

Step 2

WAIT_BPE. Waits until a BPE script (see PID IN PARAM1) of the previous day has been finished Programs:. No Bernese program is called in this step. Before it can start, it waits for PID 000 (BPE_INI) to finish.

Step 3

FCLK_CLN. Clean campaign of remaining parts from previous runs. No Bernese program is called in this step. Before it can start, it waits for PID 001 (WAIT_BPE) to finish.

Step 4

FCLK_COP. Copy required files for CLKFINAL.PCF. No Bernese program is called in this step. Before it can start, it waits for PID 002 (FCLK_CLN) to finish.

Step 5

GOTO_SRC. Jump to specified PID. No Bernese program is called in this step. Before it can start, it waits for PID 003 (FCLK_COP) to finish.

Entry point PPP

Step 6

FCLK_PPP. Copy required files from PPP. The Bernese program(s) called are OBSXTR. Before it can start, it waits for PID 009 (GOTO_SRC) to finish.OBSXTR: Inventory of the observation files from the PPP archive: $YSS+0

Step 7

CRDMRGMC. Merge coordinate files Programs: CRDMERGE. The Bernese program(s) called are CRDMERGE. Before it can start, it waits for PID 010 (FCLK_PPP) to finish.CRDMERGE: Merging coordinates/velocities

Step 8

GOTO_SOS. Skip SOS generation in case of avalable values. No Bernese program is called in this step. Before it can start, it waits for PID 011 (CRDMRGMC) to finish.

Step 9

INIT_HOI. Copy best ionosphere product for HOI correction. No Bernese program is called in this step. Before it can start, it waits for PID 012 (GOTO_SOS) to finish.

Step 10

INIT_VMF. Initialization concerning VMF setup Programs:. No Bernese program is called in this step. Before it can start, it waits for PID 013 (INIT_HOI) to finish.

Step 11

PPPESTAP. Prepare parallel GPSEST run for zero-difference files Remark : The use only phase data. The Bernese program(s) called are OBSXTR. Before it can start, it waits for PID 014 (INIT_VMF) to finish.OBSXTR: Inventory of the observation files from the PPP archive: $YSS+0

Step 12

FCLKES_P. Execute GPSEST for zero-difference files (parallel mode) Remark : Use only phase data. The Bernese program(s) called are GPSEST, SATMRK. Before it can start, it waits for PID 015 (PPPESTAP) to finish.GPSEST: $YD+0, CLOCK ESTIMATION, SOLUTION CODE+PHASE, 3D-sol ($U)SATMRK: Test: Neues Menu

Step 13

RESDEF. Last line of defence against evil PPP observation files. Screen residuals and remove bad stations. The Bernese program(s) called are GPSXTR, RESCHK, RESRMS. Before it can start, it waits for PID 016 (FCLKES_P) to finish.GPSXTR: GPSEST/ADDNEQ ExtractionRESCHK: Residual check for $YSS+0RESRMS: RESIDUAL CHECK $YD+0

Step 14

TIC_CHK. Check time intervals connected (TIC) by at least one phase observation and reject remove OBS data with unmet requirements. Programs: Remark :. No Bernese program is called in this step. Before it can start, it waits for PID 017 (RESDEF) to finish.

Step 15

GOTO_SRC. Jump to specified PID. No Bernese program is called in this step. Before it can start, it waits for PID 018 (TIC_CHK) to finish.

Entry point RINEX

PPPFINAL.PCFShow detailsPPPFINAL.PCF

PRECISE POINT POSITIONING (PPP) based on RCLKGNSS.PCF products - computes coordinates, troposphere and receiver clocks - compares GPS-only/GLONASS-only/GPS+GLONASS - compares with solutions from previous days - cleaned zero-diff. observation files are stored PPPFINAL BPE processing summary file name: PPPyyssss.PRC Remark: Different plots are generated an embbeded in a system of web-pages: - GMTKIN_P: plots related to the kinematic solution - GMTPLT_P: plots from the time series station-specific web-pages - FPPP_PRC: web-page for the most recent PPP solution - FPPP_F7D: web-page for time series of PPP solutions The web-pages and plots are located at $PATH_WWW/$CAMP_SAVPPP.

PPPFINALT.PCFShow detailsPPPFINALT.PCF

PRECISE POINT POSITIONING (PPP) based on RCLKGNSS.PCF products - computes coordinates, troposphere and receiver clocks - compares GPS-only/GLONASS-only/GPS+GLONASS - compares with solutions from previous days - cleaned zero-diff. observation files are stored PPPFINAL BPE processing summary file name: PPPyyssss.PRC Remark: Different plots are generated an embbeded in a system of web-pages: - GMTKIN_P: plots related to the kinematic solution - GMTPLT_P: plots from the time series station-specific web-pages - FPPP_PRC: web-page for the most recent PPP solution - FPPP_F7D: web-page for time series of PPP solutions The web-pages and plots are located at $PATH_WWW/$CAMP_SAVPPP.

PRECISE POINT POSITIONING (PPP) based on FCLKGNSS.PCF products - computes high-rate troposphere and slant delays PPPFINAL BPE processing summary file name: PPPyyssss.PRC

  • 17 processing steps

Skip session in case of repeation

Step 1

SKIP_SES. The BPE variable V_SKIP is assumed as the filename of the sessions protocol file. If the related file: $(V_SKIP) already exist the processing of the session comtinues at PID given as PARAM2 what is typically the end of the BPE. No Bernese program is called in this step.

Copy required files and create a priori CRD file

Step 2

FTRP_COP. Copy required files for PPPTROPO.PCF Programs: COOVEL. The Bernese program(s) called are COOVEL. Before it can start, it waits for PID 000 (SKIP_SES) to finish.COOVEL: CRD BASED ON $(CRDINF)

Step 3

FTRP_OBS. Extract the observation archive for PPPTROPO.PCF Programs:. No Bernese program is called in this step. Before it can start, it waits for PID 000 (SKIP_SES) to finish.

Step 4

INIT_HOI. Copy best ionosphere product for HOI correction. No Bernese program is called in this step. Before it can start, it waits for PID 001 (FTRP_COP) to finish.

Step 5

INIT_VMF. Initialization concerning VMF setup Programs:. No Bernese program is called in this step. Before it can start, it waits for PID 001 (FTRP_COP) to finish.

Step 6

DUMMY. Do nothing Programs:. No Bernese program is called in this step. Before it can start, it waits for PID 002 (FTRP_OBS), PID 013 (INIT_HOI), PID 014 (INIT_VMF) to finish.

Compute high-rate troposphere solution

Step 7

PPPESTXP. Prepare parallel GPSEST run for zero-difference files Remark : The use only phase data. The Bernese program(s) called are OBSXTR. Before it can start, it waits for PID 099 (DUMMY) to finish.OBSXTR: Inventory of the observation files from the PPP archive: $YSS+0

Step 8

PPPEST_P. Execute GPSEST for zero-difference files (parallel mode) Remark : The use only phase data. The Bernese program(s) called are GPSEST. Before it can start, it waits for PID 101 (PPPESTXP) to finish.GPSEST: PPP_$YSS+0_$(FFFF): PPP-based troposphere estimation

Step 9

GPSXTR. Generate summary of the paralel GPSEST runs Programs: GPSXTR. The Bernese program(s) called are GPSXTR. Before it can start, it waits for PID 102 (PPPEST_P) to finish.GPSXTR: GPSEST/ADDNEQ Extraction (Troposphere estimation)

Step 10

TROTRO. Remove non-IGS stations from code tro sinex file Programs: TROTRO. The Bernese program(s) called are TROTRO. Before it can start, it waits for PID 102 (PPPEST_P) to finish.TROTRO: Combining the troposphere SINEX files from PPP ($YSS+0)

Step 11

DUMMY. Do nothing Programs:. No Bernese program is called in this step. Before it can start, it waits for PID 111 (GPSXTR), PID 112 (TROTRO) to finish.

Step 12

DUMMY. Do nothing Programs:. No Bernese program is called in this step. Before it can start, it waits for PID 199 (DUMMY) to finish.

Step 13

FTRP_PRC. Do nothing Programs:. No Bernese program is called in this step. Before it can start, it waits for PID 900 (DUMMY) to finish.

Step 14

FTRP_SAV. Copy all relevant results from PPPTROPO.PCF to save disk Programs:. No Bernese program is called in this step. Before it can start, it waits for PID 901 (FTRP_PRC) to finish.

Step 15

FTRP_DEL. Clean campaign from PPPTROPO.PCF processing Programs:. No Bernese program is called in this step. Before it can start, it waits for PID 910 (FTRP_SAV) to finish.

Step 16

BPE_CLN. Clean BPE subdirectory. No Bernese program is called in this step. Before it can start, it waits for PID 920 (FTRP_DEL) to finish.

Step 17

DUMMY. Do nothing Programs:. No Bernese program is called in this step. Before it can start, it waits for PID 998 (BPE_CLN) to finish.

SUPERBPE.PCFShow detailsSUPERBPE.PCF

This is a skeleton for the SUPERBPE. The SUPERBPE runs a process control file (PCF) for several sessions in parallel (PID 101 and 102). The scripts to start the corresponding BPE-servers are SBPEAP resp. SBPE_P. The advantage to use a SUPERBPE in contrast to the 'Simple parallel multi session run' is that you can add other scripts before (e.g., PID 001) and/or after (e.g., PID 999) the parallel processing part.

  • 4 processing steps

Insert here script(s) to be executed before the SUPERBPE

Step 1

DUMMY. Do nothing Programs:. No Bernese program is called in this step.

SUPERBPE: Runs a PCF for several sessions in parallel

Step 2

SBPEAP. Prepares a parallel running BPE (SUPERBPE). No Bernese program is called in this step. Before it can start, it waits for PID 001 (DUMMY) to finish.

Step 3

SBPE_P. Starts a server for a parallel running BPE (SUPERBPE). No Bernese program is called in this step. Before it can start, it waits for PID 101 (SBPEAP) to finish.

Insert here script(s) to be executed after the SUPERBPE

Step 4

DUMMY. Do nothing Programs:. No Bernese program is called in this step. Before it can start, it waits for PID 102 (SBPE_P) to finish.
